Newest first. | Shattered Legacy 2026.10.20.3 | October 20, 2026 | Added | <BASEFONT COLOR=#D8D8D8>- The League runs a requisitions board. Every guild posts three requests a day for a magic item it wants: a sword with Hit Chance for the Warriors, a spellbook with Lower Mana Cost for the Arcane Society, an undead slayer for the Keepers, a slayer instrument for the Bards, and so on. Found or crafted both count. Turn one in at the board in the New Haven field office, at the guild's New Haven chapter (the guildmaster's Requisitions button), at the League Registrar or at the Appraiser, and the item is taken for Renown, that guild's standing and gold. Newest first. | Shattered Legacy 2026.10.20.3 | October 20, 2026 | Added | <BASEFONT COLOR=#D8D8D8>- The League runs a requisitions board. Every guild posts three requests a day for a magic item it wants: a sword with Hit Chance for the Warriors, a spellbook with Lower Mana Cost for the Arcane Society, an undead slayer for the Keepers, a slayer instrument for the Bards, and so on. Found or crafted both count. Turn one in at the board in the New Haven field office, at the guild's New Haven chapter (the guildmaster's Requisitions button), at the League Registrar or at the Appraiser, and the item is taken for Renown, that guild's standing and gold. Unfilled requests simply roll over at 05:00 UTC; nothing is lost and nothing is owed.</BASEFONT> | <BASEFONT COLOR=#D8D8D8>- Requests grow with your rank in that guild, and your League rank sets how high they can go.</BASEFONT> | <BASEFONT COLOR=#D8D8D8>- The Appraiser, a League clerk in the New Haven field office and by the banks of Britain, Trinsic, Minoc, Vesper, Moonglow, Skara Brae, Luna and the Royal City, buys any magic item no request wants. The price follows how many properties the item has and how strong they are, near what a shopkeeper pays. Drop an item on the Appraiser for a quote, or ask which of your requests wants it.</BASEFONT> | Previous | Page 9 of 62 | Next | Close
